Pronunciation and numerical values are derived from the Braille alphabets. Structure and system of scripts resembles combination of Tibetan, Tengwar, Korean, Arabic. It has carbon-based lifeform. Plant is violet due to uses bacteriorhodopsin alternative to chlorophyll. Planet is made of silicate, iron, asolate, titanium. Planet has highly abundance of diamond, tungsten, radioactive elements, similar to fascist planet Frenya. Planet is most envied by other extraterrestrial civilizations, it has extreme abundance of other elements from neutron star. Atmosphere is composed nitrogen, oxygen, asole dioxide, trace neon. Atmosphere is lavender-colored due to presence of asole dioxide. Ocean is primarily composed of water, with small quantities of salt and asole dissolved within it.
